Appendix — Quick Reference

A.1 Role Legend

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  Code     Meaning
  -------- --------------------------------------------------------------
  ES       Executive Sponsor — owns the business case and clears
           organizational barriers.

  CM       Change Manager — owns the journi project, the four framework
           readings, and the adoption plan.

  PM       Program / Project Manager — owns scope, schedule, budget and
           delivery milestones.

  FPO      Functional Process Owner — the business SME who owns the
           future-state process, system, or control.

  ITL      IT / Technical Lead — owns configuration, data, integration
           and technical build.

  SUP      Frontline Supervisor / People Manager — coaches the cohort day
           to day.

  EU       End User / Impacted Employee cohort — performs the process or
           is subject to the change.
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------

A.2 RACSI Legend

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  Code     Meaning
  -------- --------------------------------------------------------------
  R        Responsible — does the work. More than one role may be
           Responsible for the same Task.

  A        Accountable — owns the outcome; exactly one role per Task.

  C        Consulted — two-way input sought before/during the Task.

  S        Support — provides resources or assistance without owning the
           Task.

  I        Informed — kept up to date, one-way.
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------

A.3 Project / Change Tag Legend

  -----------------------------------------------------------------------
  Tag         Meaning
  ----------- -----------------------------------------------------------
  [PROJECT]   Delivery-owned: scope, schedule, budget, technical build,
              data, cutover.

  [CHANGE]    Adoption-owned: awareness, desire, capability coaching,
              sentiment, identity/transition work.

  [JOINT]     Cannot be meaningfully split — a delivery decision and an
              adoption decision are fused into one action.
  -----------------------------------------------------------------------

A.4 Glossary

-   Common Transformation Lifecycle — the seven-phase spine (P1–P7) this
    guide shows is shared across all six transformation types, from
    Intake & Diagnosis through Sustainment & Closure.

-   Target Operating Model (TOM) — the blueprint of an organization's
    future structure, governance, decision rights and capabilities,
    produced in an Operating Model Redesign.

-   RPA (Robotic Process Automation) — software “bots” that execute
    repetitive, rules-based digital tasks, one of the four actor types
    explicitly designed in a Business Process Automation Full Track.

-   Full Track (Business Process Automation) — an automation design that
    explicitly orchestrates RPA bots, AI/ML models, human judgment
    steps, and system integrations within one workflow, rather than
    automating a single task in isolation.

-   Mock-up Audit — an internal or third-party pre-assessment run
    exactly like a real certification audit, used in an Integrated
    Management System program to surface nonconformities before the real
    certifying audit.

-   Certifying Audit — the external, accredited audit whose pass/fail
    decision determines whether a management system achieves
    certification against a standard (e.g., ISO 9001, 45001, 14001).

-   Surveillance Audit — a periodic (commonly annual) external audit
    that confirms a certified management system remains compliant after
    initial certification.

-   Attestation — a formal, often signed, confirmation that an
    individual has received required training or acknowledged a policy,
    used as compliance evidence.

-   Center of Excellence (CoE) — a standing, permanent team that owns
    ongoing governance of an automation platform (bots, AI models,
    integrations) after a Business Process Automation program closes.

-   Composite Readiness Index (CRI) — journi's blended 0–100 readiness
    score (ADKAR 50%, Kübler-Ross sentiment 25%, training completion
    25%); used identically across all six transformation types.

-   Divergence Pattern — strong ADKAR Knowledge/Ability paired with a
    Bridges reading still at “Ending”; journi's one boolean
    cross-framework alert, applicable to any transformation type.
